# C5 matrix, disposition and provenance audit

Date: 2026-11-20. Author: W412 (Codex implementer), under [TASK W412](../../work/v1.8.0-root-queue/TASK-W412-MATRIX-PROVENANCE-AUDIT.md). This is an evidence report, not a Gate, requirement, version, originality or visual approval. No source, manifest, ADR, matrix or status file was changed.

## 1. Identity, scope and method

- Audited candidate: `9172113ad4ecfb9047432f02b97d240fc06130b6`.
- Auditing checkout: `81f4167bf3dfb93a47c50389c710c7144d24eb6c`, branch `codex/v1.8.0-w412`, initially clean. `git diff --name-only 9172113ad HEAD -- docs/PORTING_MATRIX.md docs/provenance src/main/resources src/generated src/imported docs/work/v1.8.0-content-ledger.csv` was empty. Registry sources were also unchanged. Thus later documentation commits did not substitute different candidate resource or ledger bytes.
- JAR: `D:/GitHub/ARCE-Task-Evidence/root-queue/C5/candidate/unpacked/advancedrocketry-community-1.20.1-1.8.0-dev.jar`; SHA-256 `e5cd2beb4a38b4ea741c5103b18ba224d3f102ef6231adbc3a0849ad4f1e35c8` (matches the task).
- Inventory: `D:/GitHub/ARCE-Task-Evidence/final-campaign/9172113ad/fixtures/K13/assets.csv`; SHA-256 `3f3faf076a7118e83881c9ca3306adb6f236ebd6b78ec288781a08e04a9ce079`. All **908** rows have unique paths and match the JAR's SHA-256 and uncompressed byte count. Every non-directory `assets/` member is represented; the additional row is the root logo. No duplicate JAR member name was found.
- Requirements: [version §§3, 5, 9, 14, 15](../../versions/V1.8.0-CLASSIC-CONTENT-COMPLETION.md), [requirement map](../../releases/v1.8.0/REQUIREMENT-MAP.md) and [draft Gate status](../../releases/v1.8.0/GATE-STATUS.md). The acceptance cursor and unapproved prerequisite releases are unchanged.

The inventory was joined to allowed source roots by **path and exact bytes**, then to JSON provenance namings using the existing audit's supersession/history rules, not filename resemblance. Markdown scope declarations and the linked v1.4 inventories were examined separately; they are not silently promoted to schema-2 manifests or human approvals. Appendix A enumerates every asset, including unused textures. A campaign sampling exclusion does not exempt a shipped file from provenance.

The existing `audit_repository(Path('.'), candidate_jar)` function in [the provenance validator](../../../scripts/validate_v180_provenance.py) was run in memory from `python -B -`, without its output-writing CLI. ZIP inspection, source reads, registry string checks and ledger checks did not start a JVM, server or client. Class-entry/constant presence is **static packaging evidence**, not decompilation or a runtime registry dump. No behavior, survival reachability, native restart, V1/V2 or performance acceptance is claimed.

## 2. Findings and closure actions

Severity uses [quality budgets §3](../../17-V1PLUS-QUALITY-BUDGETS.md). The Medium classifications below concern unresolved release evidence/scope, not a demonstrated runtime crash or exploit. There is no new Critical/High finding from this limited audit; that is not a candidate-wide zero-Critical/High verdict.

### MP-01 — Medium: five agreed-scope units remain undelivered in the authoritative ledger

The main matrix contains no literal `MISSING`, but ADR-062 §1 explicitly treats `PLANNED` units as MISSING until delivery. Strict closure exits 1: `closure: 5 ledger rows are still PLANNED`. All five are inside v1.8, not covered by its deferred/rejected decisions:

| Ledger unit | Batch / intended modern ID | C5 observation |
|---|---|---|
| `block_variant:loader/1` | `C17c` / `advancedrocketrycommunity:rocket_satellite_bay` | Registered source declaration and packaged blockstate/model exist; delivery evidence cell remains empty. |
| `block_variant:loader/2` | `C17a` / `advancedrocketrycommunity:rocket_item_unloader` | Registered source declaration and packaged blockstate/model exist; delivery evidence cell remains empty. |
| `block_variant:loader/3` | `C17a` / `advancedrocketrycommunity:rocket_item_loader` | Registered source declaration and packaged blockstate/model exist; delivery evidence cell remains empty. |
| `block_variant:loader/4` | `C17a` / `advancedrocketrycommunity:rocket_fluid_unloader` | Registered source declaration and packaged blockstate/model exist; delivery evidence cell remains empty. |
| `block_variant:loader/5` | `C17a` / `advancedrocketrycommunity:rocket_fluid_loader` | Registered source declaration and packaged blockstate/model exist; delivery evidence cell remains empty. |


Evidence: [ledger](../../work/v1.8.0-content-ledger.csv), [ModBlocks](../../../src/main/java/io/github/sunthemoon/advancedrocketrycommunity/registry/ModBlocks.java) (lines 440–468), and [C5 switch report](../../work/v1.8.0-root-queue/records/C5-SWITCH-REPORT.md). The five reserved IDs do not prove their gated workflows are production-admitted. This is an existing open delivery condition, independently reproduced here, not a new missing-code allegation.

**Closes with:** Root reconciles each row to committed C5 delivery evidence and the separately reserved owner decisions/admission boundary. If scope changes instead, an accepted ADR revision must name the item and player impact. Do not just relabel PLANNED, enable switches or count the resource files as acceptance.

### MP-02 — Medium: final generated/NEW provenance coverage is weaker than the complete batch-manifest requirement

**596/908 assets have no exact per-file JSON naming in `docs/provenance`** (533 in the v1.8 generated root; 63 inherited). This is **not** a claim that all 596 have unknown authorship: many have coherent Markdown NEW declarations, and six v1.4 assets (four visual profiles and two language files) have matching supplementary inventory pins. Appendix A distinguishes those cases from generation-only evidence and records every shipped hash. Missing a hash in a Markdown declaration is not itself a hash mismatch.

There nevertheless are shipped NEW artwork cohorts for which the required final batch record is absent. Concrete examples are `assets/advancedrocketrycommunity/textures/block/observatory.png` (SHA-256 `8c02528c222690d9700991c6a8222ba37d05bccf7d252da4b4f5f15297ae98c4`) and `astrobody_data_processor.png` (`a55354280cc898f1ce3028297022c5657d78262b52ea1176b7bf94cb6a4a060e`). Their source is original code in [V180ResearchArt](../../../src/main/java/io/github/sunthemoon/advancedrocketrycommunity/datagen/V180ResearchArt.java), but no per-file JSON naming or scoped record in `docs/provenance` was found for that research-art cohort. A Java “Original MIT” comment and a generated-root match are not the batch review record required by [ADR-061 §§4.3–4.5](../../decisions/ADR-061-CLASSIC-CONTENT-IDENTITY-IMPORT-AND-VALIDATION.md).

Other explicit partial examples are [the tank receipt](../../provenance/v1.8.0-c16a-tank-new-resources.md) and [the pump receipt](../../provenance/v1.8.0-c16a-pump-new-resources.md): they identify NEW source/scope, but defer final generated hashes/review rather than provide an approved final batch manifest. Ordinary generated JSON is allowed by ADR-061; the defect is incomplete final batch traceability/review, not the mere use of DataGen.

**Closes with:** Root reconciles Appendix A's generation-only and scope-only entries to committed final NEW/GENERATED batch records (source, MIT license, authoring/base identity, applicable target hashes and review), including existing records outside `docs/provenance` where legitimately referenced. Resolve genuine missing cohorts rather than treating all 596 as imports or inventing upstream identities. Preserve historical receipts and evidence.

These JSON records contain source/target hashes and MIT NEW/GENERATED declarations, but **no `review` block**. The asset hashes match; approval is a different check.

ADR-061 §4.5 requires approval of every record for G0. The [November 8 approvals](../../work/v1.8.0-root-queue/records/OWNER-DECISIONS-20261108.md) explicitly cover W275/W277 and the earlier D7 set; they are not blanket approval of these later records. The mechanical provenance validator does not enforce approval completeness.

**Closes with:** locate any actual owner/reviewer decision covering the exact records and record its bounded approval with the proper digest, or obtain the missing review. A code-review result, passing target hash or switch decision alone must not be substituted for that decision.

### MP-05 — Low: two broad disposition rows lack an inline deciding ADR and precise scope

`ASM/coremod` (REJECTED) and `旧世界直开` (DEFERRED, “v1.x research”) cite no ADR in the matrix. Existing policy does address them: AGENTS §3.3 prohibits migration of coremods; ADR-062 §4 names the rejected non-living gravity hook, while §3 defers seated cargo access and §2 redesigns lifecycle/flight hooks. The implementation mechanism is rejected, **not every behavior formerly implemented through ASM**. [ADR-012, Save compatibility](../../decisions/ADR-012-V090-BETA-COMPATIBILITY-CONTRACT.md) explicitly says direct 1.12.2 world loading is unsupported; [roadmap §6](../../16-POST-1.0-VERSION-ROADMAP.md) discusses possible offline conversion without committing to a v1.x delivery.

**Closes with:** add the deciding references and distinguish mechanism rejection from behavior dispositions; align direct-loading wording with the unsupported boundary. If a dated migration/research commitment is intended, its deciding ADR must actually name that commitment. No new ADR or scope decision was made by W412.

## 3. Matrix and disposition reconciliation (14.1 / 14.2)

The main matrix uses implementation states, not final parity verdicts. Its **79 data rows** count as follows:

| State | Count |
|---|---|
| PASSED | 26 |
| IN_PROGRESS | 23 |
| PLANNED | 9 |
| BLOCKED | 1 |
| DEFERRED | 9 |
| REJECTED | 11 |
| UNKNOWN | 0 |
| MISSING | 0 |
| NOT_AUDITED | 0 |
| AUDITED | 0 |


There are no literal UNKNOWN or MISSING rows to list. In the detailed **653-unit** ledger there are 214 IMPLEMENTED, 257 REDESIGNED, 94 MERGED, 25 DEFERRED, 58 REJECTED and 5 PLANNED. ADR-062's historical section-1 counts are contract-baseline counts, not current delivery totals. The five effective MISSING units are all listed in MP-01. No additional literal UNKNOWN/MISSING unit was found. Absence of a literal token alone does not establish 14.1 or 14.2 acceptance.

All **83** detailed DEFERRED/REJECTED units cite ADR-062 and map to its explicit player-impact groups; Appendix D enumerates them. This semantic comparison is additional to the ledger validator's link/form checks. Feature-only decisions such as star-map overlay, cross-system cargo, directional thrust and the legacy gravity API need their main-matrix rows even when they are not separate legacy inventory units.

### 3.2 Registry/data versus matrix

C5 contains 134 blockstate files and 251 item-model files. They are not 385 necessarily distinct registered IDs: block items share names, technical blocks have no item, and model files are not registration evidence. In the unchanged registration sources, all **61 direct literal BLOCKS registrations** and **35 direct literal ITEMS registrations** have their literal IDs in their corresponding C5 registry class constant pools. Dynamic material/port/helper registrations were not treated as absent just because a literal regex does not enumerate them.

No confirmed missing registration/resource was found for the matrix's 26 historical PASSED slices, and no new unowned gameplay family was found. Modern IDs such as `sealed_rocket_block`, `gravity_field_controller`, `orbital_laser_drill`, the machine ports and the material products lack individual main-matrix lines because that table groups them under rocket lifecycle, area gravity, laser drill, multiblocks/classic machines and materials. They are not orphan findings based on a string-search alone; the [per-unit ledger](../../work/v1.8.0-content-ledger.csv) supplies the finer mapping. Internal adapters, menus, registry serializers and test classes do not each require a legacy-feature row.

The concrete implementation-status disagreements are MP-04. Historical PASSED rows are explicitly limited to their earlier vertical slices by the matrix footer; e.g. the v0.1 language and five minimal recipe slices do not assert all v1.8 language/art/recipes are accepted. The broader v1.8 rows remain IN_PROGRESS. This audit cannot prove a runtime registry census or behavioral parity by ZIP inspection.

## 4. Asset provenance reconciliation (9 / 14.3 / 15)

The 908 inventoried files comprise 1 root logo, 134 blockstates, 6 other assets (visual profiles), 32 language files, 23 OBJ, 23 MTL, 134 block models, 251 item models, 1 sound definition, 11 OGG and 292 textures.

| Evidence class | Assets | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| Exact JSON naming | 310 | JAR SHA-256 equals the resolved target hash; 88 UPSTREAM_AR_MIT, 166 NEW, 26 GENERATED and 30 COMMUNITY_AUTHORED_DATAGEN. |
| Shared v1.8 language | 2 | 28 historical namings checked by key, all successful. Their old whole-file snapshot hashes are not the candidate hash. |
| No per-file JSON naming in docs/provenance | 596 | Generation origin is known; Markdown scope declarations / supplementary inventories are distinguished in Appendix A. Not 596 proven unknown-source imports. |


All 88 currently resolved UPSTREAM_AR_MIT asset entries name `https://github.com/Advanced-Rocketry/AdvancedRocketry`, commit `c5cd5af62fc07cd4e0d24f06a16033f181c47c04`, and MIT. This includes transformed model/MTL records: an upstream source hash need not equal a transformed target hash. The JSON entries record transformations and source hashes; Appendix B exposes their source identities. Some repeated upstream-derived bytes appear as both model and block-atlas texture paths, so the validator's 77 raw legacy-hash hits are not the 88 upstream provenance entries.

**Current applicable target-hash disagreements: 0. Missing asset paths named by resolved JSON records: 0.** There are **26 historical source-root asset copies whose bytes no longer ship under that JAR name**; Appendix E lists both hashes and the current source. They remain in immutable historical generated roots and are intentionally overridden during packaging. A literal comparison of all historical records to the JAR would falsely report them as corrupt. Similarly, superseded records of the same target and shared-language snapshots are not fresh mismatches.

The scoped Markdown declarations can contain actual hashes (e.g. analyzer and thermite). Where an identified scope record contains the shipped hash, Appendix A says `MD hash match`; `scope only` means no applicable target pin was established. A pre-authoring declaration is not silently changed into final review approval. Six matching v1.4 inventory entries (four visual profiles and the two latest discovery/migration language files) are also noted. Older environmental/navigation/sky language snapshots differ and are historical, not applicable current whole-file pins.

| Check / tool | Actual result and boundary |

Identity, unofficial statements, retained MIT notice, fixed upstream commit, Markdown links, forbidden legacy/binary and case checks; bootstrap/v0.1 provenance and historical generated inventories; resource/localization references and historical evidence checks. It does NOT call the v1.8 candidate JAR audit or prove current G0 approval. |
| [validate_v180_provenance.py](../../../scripts/validate_v180_provenance.py): rule 1 | 1,936 assets/data entries match exactly one permitted resource root; successful. Root logo checked separately here. |

Asset coverage is 89 IMPORTED, 444 REGENERATE, 365 EXCLUDE; 0 IMPORT/REVIEW. The semantic meaning of ADR links remains a human/read-only audit responsibility. |


These successful mechanical checks are observations, not new findings and not Gate decisions. They do not check every NEW batch's review block, prove that every generated file has an individual schema-2 entry, or establish originality of code-drawn pixels.

## 5. Licenses, notices and source boundaries

[NOTICE.md](../../../NOTICE.md) accurately attributes the MIT upstream and new MIT community work and states the unofficial/Minecraft/Forge disclaimers. [UPSTREAM.md](../../../UPSTREAM.md) pins the same upstream branch and commit as the 88 imported/derived entries and excludes unreviewed forks/LibVulpes.

All five required/legal notice members below exist and are **byte-identical** to the tracked source copies:

| JAR member | Tracked source | JAR SHA-256 |
|---|---|---|
| `META-INF/LICENSE` | [LICENSE](../../../LICENSE) | `6cb93bf243331e49b6349d24e884e6cf27f8f444d66bf4bd00f9469e8a6f37ba` |
| `META-INF/NOTICE.md` | [NOTICE.md](../../../NOTICE.md) | `4cc293cee77bbbd95b81b2b666eddb0f5428ca782b17dfcc51075a8fc1ac7d36` |
| `META-INF/THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` | [THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md](../../../THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md) | `2fd334dc8c2c8a2d4cd9f6649694a41f7bacfea33a6f1084f62a01728ae66364` |
| `META-INF/licenses/GRADLE-8.1.1-LICENSE.txt` | [docs/licenses/GRADLE-8.1.1-LICENSE.txt](../../licenses/GRADLE-8.1.1-LICENSE.txt) | `e5bfcf1132c8e12c3fce87d4dfbcb543cfb7202d8fa28ba85c07132e30836437` |
| `META-INF/licenses/MINECRAFT-FORGE-1.20.1-47.4.10-LICENSE.txt` | [docs/licenses/MINECRAFT-FORGE-1.20.1-47.4.10-LICENSE.txt](../../licenses/MINECRAFT-FORGE-1.20.1-47.4.10-LICENSE.txt) | `481c96d94d182382c4225d5b210f8c658c85350cf548f25c9f56c058804f1e57` |


The MIT copy retains `Copyright (c) 2017` and community attribution. The Forge/Gradle supplemental copies are expected under [THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md](../../../THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md), not unexplained non-MIT art. That record approves the MDK-derived `pack.mcmeta` and `META-INF/mods.toml` treatment and requires the companion source offering; W412 examined the binary only and does not assert that the final distribution already offers its sources classifier.

No embedded dependency JAR, `assets/minecraft/` file, font or shader file was found. Every class entry is beneath `io/github/sunthemoon/advancedrocketrycommunity/` (4,707 classes). Every asset namespace is this project's main or version-suffixed namespace; no LibVulpes/fork asset namespace is shipped. Native model, particle, sound and texture **references** are not copied official resources and are not findings. Legal notice copies/MDK metadata are the separately recorded bootstrap exception; “only MIT upstream or NEW” describes gameplay/assets, not those licensed bootstrap files.

No positive evidence of forbidden third-party/official artwork or an unrecorded upstream import was found. Exact imported-byte attribution is established; **a universal originality/approval assertion is withheld because MP-02/MP-03 remain open**. Namespace, hash screening and NEW declarations alone cannot prove an image's authorship.

## 6. Commands, limits and handoff

- `python -B scripts/validate_repository.py`: exit 0, **45 passed, 0 pending, 0 warnings, 0 failed** (initial clean-tree run; rerun after documentation editing is recorded in W412).

## Appendix A. Exhaustive C5 asset-to-record index

Each row is one of the 908 verified inventory entries. All displayed asset SHA-256 values are from the JAR and equal the inventory; the source-resource link points at the same bytes. Upstream source hashes are distinct from target hashes where a transformation is recorded. `MATCH` means bytes match a record, **not a requirement/Gate PASS**.

Record/source codes are defined in Appendix B. `K` denotes shared-language key validation (28 vouchers, Appendix C). `GEN` denotes only placement in a community-generated root (or inherited project source), with MIT origin not individually pinned by a JSON entry. A linked Markdown cohort is a scoped NEW declaration, not a schema-2 approval. `NO SCOPED RECORD` identifies entries for which this audit did not establish a narrower declaration; it does not assert the generator copied third-party material.
